My wife says it’s just talk, but I know they’re telling me to be careful.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like people are talking about you or sending you messages that others don't seem to notice?\n\nPatient: Yes... sometimes. The men at the domino table, they say things that feel like they’re meant for me. And the radio too, now and then. My wife says it’s just talk, but... I think they’re warning me to be careful.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel that certain people or voices are specifically trying to communicate with you or give you special messages that others aren’t meant to understand?\n\nPatient: Yes… I think so. Mostly the men at the domino table, and sometimes the radio announcer. They say little things, not plain, but I can tell they’re meant for me… warnings to be careful. My wife says it’s just talk, but I don’t think so.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever feel like the thoughts you're having are not entirely your own, or that someone is putting thoughts into your mind?\n\nPatient: I don’t know about someone putting thoughts in my head… it’s more that the men at the domino table, and sometimes the radio man, say things that are meant for me. Little warnings. My wife says it’s just talk, but… I know they’re telling me to be careful.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever feel like things that are happening around you—like things on TV, in the news, or things people say—are directly related to you, even if they don’t seem connected to others?\n\nPatient: Yes… that’s why I came, really. The men at the domino table, and sometimes the radio announcer… they say little things. My wife says it’s just talk, but I know they’re meant for me. Like warnings, to be careful.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like things that happen in the world, like events or things people say, are specially connected to you in a way that others don’t understand?\n\nPatient: Yes… I think so. Like I said, those men at the domino table, and sometimes the radio man… they say little things. My wife says it’s just talk, but I know it’s meant for me. Like they’re warning me to be careful.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever feel like things around you—like objects, events, or even people’s actions—are directly influencing your thoughts or that they have a special meaning for you that others don’t see?\n\nPatient: Yes… I mean, not objects so much. It’s more what people say. The men at the domino table, and sometimes the radio announcer… they give little messages. My wife says it’s just talk, but I know they’re meant for me. Like warnings to be careful.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel that only certain people, like the men at the domino table or the radio announcer, are sending you these messages, or do you feel like this happens with many people or things around you in general?\n\nPatient: Mostly those men… and sometimes the radio announcer. Not everybody. It’s just certain things they say, like little messages for me… warnings to be careful. My wife says it’s nothing, but… I know what I hear.\n\nDoctor: Are the warnings from the men at the domino table and the radio announcer the only instances where you feel messages are being sent to you, or do you notice other people or situations also giving you similar messages?\n\nPatient: Mostly just them… the men at the domino table, and sometimes the radio announcer. That’s why I came, really. They say little things meant for me, like warnings to be careful. My wife says it’s just talk, but… I know what I hear.\n\nDoctor: Do you believe that these messages are part of something bigger, like a special mission or a hidden plot that only you can understand?\n\nPatient: I don’t know about a big mission… I wouldn’t say that. It’s just… those men at the domino table, and sometimes the radio, they say things meant for me. Warnings, mostly. My wife says it’s just talk, but I know what I hear.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever feel that other people, besides the men at the domino table or the radio announcer, are also sending you hidden messages or trying to communicate with you in a special way?\n\nPatient: No… not really other people. My wife says it’s just talk, but I know what I hear.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like these messages are the only things that have special meaning to you, or do you also notice other things in your daily life that seem to have hidden meanings just for you?\n\nPatient: It’s mostly just those messages… the men at the domino table, and sometimes the radio announcer. I came in because of that. They say little things meant for me, like warning me to be careful. My wife says it’s just talk, but… I know what I hear.\n\nDoctor: Do you know who is sending these messages to you, or do you wonder about where they are coming from?\n\nPatient: I don’t really know who’s behind it.
